### Page 4

AI server: Robust demand outlook, Rubin generation ramp on schedule

### Page 5

Robust AI server demand outlook supported by strong Capex spending
 We model 45% AI chip shipment CAGR in 2025-28.
 GPU server demand remains robust this year on resilient US CSP AI spending and pent-up Neocloud/enterprise demand.
 We see longer demand visibility into next year on strong demand outlook and sustained U.S. CSP capex spending growth.
 We forecast a 60% CAGR for AI ASIC chip shipments in 2025-28, driven by strong AWS Trainium/Google TPU demand.

AI server shipment forecast
Source: J.P. Morgan estimates for all years, including historical. Note: Total AI server includes AI ASIC + high-end GPU AI servers.

### Page 6

Upbeat 2026 AI capex outlook, extended visibility into 2027
 U.S. hyperscalers raised 2026 capex outlook and provided positive commentary on 2027.
 JPM U.S. hardware team now forecast +80%/+50% YoY growth of datacenter capex in 2026/27, vs. previous estimates of +63%/+40% YoY,
which is the third raise this year. We see ~10% upside to the estimates after the recent updated outlook for leading hyperscalers.
 While negative FCF among leading hyperscalers over the next few quarters is inevitable, we see a few positive indicators to support continued AI

investments, including robust core businesses, strong AI demand indicators, and decent returns of AI businesses with continued improvement.
 Datacenter capex from Tier 2 providers (e.g. emerging neocloud and other data center-related companies) is now becoming a much more significant
cohort.
 We expect the group to grow capex by 65%/39% YoY to US$90bn/US$130bn in 2026/2027.
 We estimate US$5.5tn of AI capex over 2026-30, with US$4.1tn funded through debt. We expect the IG corporate debt market to finance more than

US$2.1tn of data center investments over the next five years
Top 4 US CSP/ISP cloud capex trend

### Page 8

90% of APAC respondents on board with AI, with upside in spending in the near future
 JPM Asia Pacific Artificial Intelligence Implementation Survey 2026 (link) suggests that AI investment is already mainstream, with 88% of APAC
respondents reporting AI expenditure over the last 12 months, while only ~3% said they had not invested and ~7% were unsure. Key findings include:
 AI investment is mainstream and growing fast, but leadership is uneven.

 AI stated value case is productivity-led but with a growth mindset; so far cost reduction is secondary.
 Execution capability will increasingly separate AI winners; most respondents were optimistic about profit implications.
 Average AI spend is expected to rise from 4.5% of expenses plus capex over the last 12 months to 5.8% over the next 12 months.
AI expenditure/investment (last 12 months) by region
Source: JPM Asia Pacific Artificial Intelligence Implementation Survey 2026 (link). Source: JPM Asia Pacific Artificial Intelligence Implementation Survey 2026 (link).

### Page 9

Nvidia GPU roadmap: Rubin on schedule, Kyber architecture facing challenges
 Vera Rubin system ramps up on schedule in late 3Q to 4Q26
 Our research indicates limited impact on the Vera Rubin ramp schedule from the recent leading PCB maker yield issue.
 We estimate 70-80k NVL72 rack shipments this year, comprising 60-65k GB300 rack shipments and ~10k VR rack shipments.
 We continue to forecast ~1.5mn Rubin chip production this year (board level output), but expect only ~10k VR NVL72 rack shipments due to

production cycle time and component shortages.
 We expect 85-95k NVL72 rack shipments in 2027, with VR200 rack being the majority.
NVL72 ODM rack quarterly shipments (k units)
Nvidia GPU server roadmap
Source: J.P. Morgan, Company data.

### Page 10

Deep dive into NVL72 BOM cost: GPU and Memory to be the key cost driver
 We estimate the ODM price of the GB300/VR200 rack to be ~US$4.1/6.5mn, ~20%/90% higher than that of the GB200 server, primarily driven by
GPU and memory price increases and content upgrades in the networking components and power and thermal systems.
 We expect AI server ODM GMs to decline to 3.6%/2.7% of GB300/VR200 vs. 4.2% in GB200.
 However, we see MVA dollars (GP dollars per rack) increasing in VR200, due to a more complex server architecture and longer testing time.

 We estimate ~US$148k/ $175k for the MVA of GB300/VR200 vs. ~US$145k for GB200.
NVL72 BoM - Summary table
Source: J.P. Morgan estimates.

### Page 11

Secular ASIC server demand
 We forecast AI ASIC chips to grow 77%/88% YoY in 2026/2027, mainly driven by strong AWS Trainium and Google TPU demand.
 As AI inference activities scale, we expect more end customers to develop its own AI inference ASIC solutions, which could further expand the AI
ASIC server TAM.
JPM ASIC chip/server estimates
Source: J.P. Morgan estimates.

### Page 12

Consolidated market landscape in AI server ODM space
 Hon Hai and Quanta stand out as preferred picks in the NVL72 supply chain, while Celestica and Wiwynn lead among ASIC plays.
 While we see tier-2 ODMs (e.g. Pegatron, Compal) managing to break into the space, complex design capabilities, frequent product upgrades, and
high working capital requirements should keep the competitive landscape concentrated among a few major players.

### Page 13

General Servers: Multi-year strong demand driven by AI, key swing factor on supply constraints

### Page 14

Agentic AI CPUs becoming a new driver for server CPU demand
 As we migrate towards the era of agentic AI, focusing on test-time scaling (i.e. long thinking), CPUs are playing an increasingly important role in
orchestration, running tools and skills, storage, and security.
 For example, the Vera CPU not only sits in the NVL72 rack systems, but also serves as a standalone CPU across the entire Vera Rubin platform,
including storage servers and CPU servers.

 We attribute the strong general server demand this year (30-40% YoY growth on our estimates) primarily to the emerging demand for agentic AI.
Source: Nvidia.
Agent = LLM + Harness; CPU plays a critical role in the agentic AI ecosystem

### Page 15

Accelerating server CPU growth cycle on AI inference cycle
 We segment CPU demand into three buckets—(1) AI server headnode CPUs, (2) regular server CPUs, and (3) agentic-AI CPUs.
 As AI applications transition from training toward inference, we expect agentic-AI and AI headnode CPU demand to drive a “super” general server
CPU cycle.
 We forecast total server CPU shipments to grow from 26mn units in 2025 to 68mn by 2028 (38% CAGR) based on a top-down methodology,

triangulated with bottom-up estimates.
 We forecast a 74%/5%/155% CAGR of AI server headnode CPU/ regular server CPU/ agentic AI server CPU demand in 2025-28.
 We forecast a 53% CAGR of server CPU revenue TAM in 2025-2028, driven by the fast-growing server CPU demand and a ~10% CAGR of server
CPU ASP expansion.
Source: J.P. Morgan estimates. Gartner® data for 2024 total server CPU shipments. Global AI accelerator shipments are based on our CoWoS estimates by the semi team.

JPM server CPU forecast - top-down methodology

### Page 17

Super server cycle underpinned by the recent uptick in x86 CPU vendor revenue growth
 We have seen accelerating growth in Intel/AMD server datacenter CPU revenue growth in the past three quarters, driven by strong server demand.
 AMD also cast an upbeat long-term outlook for server CPU TAM (US$220bn by 2030 or ~50%+ CAGR in 2025-2030), which underpins our research
of the multi-year super server cycle across hyperscalers and the positive demand signals across the supply chain.

Source: J.P. Morgan U.S. semi team estimates, Company data. Note: AMD's datacenter revenue
excludes GPU revenue.

### Page 18

An AI-led multi-year general server upcycle
 1Q26 U.S. CSP server shipments were better-than-seasonal at up QoQ and the overall server shipments appear to have grown double-digit QoQ in
2Q26.
 Initial outlook indicates server shipments should continue to grow into 2H26, led by U.S. CSP server demand.
 We have picked up incremental positive signs in server demand over the past 3-6 months with order visibility extended into next year.
 Lotes and ASPEED’s order visibility has already extended into 2027. Memory vendors also collected 50-80% demand growth feedback from the

U.S. CSPs for next year. The key swing factor is supply chain constraints.
 We anticipate an accelerating shipment growth of 25% YoY next year, driven by accumulated backlog from this year and strong server demand.
 By customer type, we forecast 40-50% growth/ single digit decline to flattish for CSPs/enterprise server shipments in 2026/27, respectively.
 In the longer run, we expect server shipments to sustain at double digit growth in 2028-29, driven by the multi-year AI-led server demand.

Source: Gartner®, J.P. Morgan estimates.
Server quarterly shipments, QoQ and YoY trend
Source: Gartner®, J.P. Morgan estimates.
Server annual shipments and YoY trend

### Page 24

Datacenter Networking: Whitebox Switching Enters a New Growth Cycle

### Page 25

AI-driven Networking upgrades expand whitebox switching market opportunity
 We estimate ~US$6bn datacenter networking serviceable addressable market (SAM including generic ethernet switch, AI back-end and front-end
networking) for the top 4 CSPs for Accton in 2025 and expect the market to grow at a ~75% CAGR to reach ~ $33bn by 2028, driven by
 Strong AI-driven general purpose server demand
 AI front-end and back-end networking upgrades
 AI-led accelerating network upgrade cycles

Source: J.P. Morgan estimates, Gartner®. Note: Calculation of Accton’s datacenter switch SAM only includes whitebox ToR switches for scale-out fabrics. AI ASIC scale-out
networking switch SAM only includes AWS ASIC back-end ToR switch TAM, while we include AI front-end scale-out SAM into generic whitebox Ethernet switch categories..
Accton’s datacenter switch revenue SAM and YoY growth

### Page 26

AI-driven general purpose server demand sets the stage for switching growth
 We expect general server shipments to accelerate over the next few years on AI-driven inference demand.
 We estimate CSP’s general server shipment growth of 40%/25%/20% in 2026/27/28.
 There could be upside risks to our general server shipment assumptions driven by fast-growing agentic AI demand.
 Over the past 2–3 years, CSPs have prioritized AI server investment and data center build-outs, while networking has lagged.

 Gartner® data shows ODM-direct server spending increased 207% / 127% in 2024/25 versus ODM direct switch spending growth of 18%/103%
over the same period.
 We expect CSPs to step up networking spending to reduce latency and improve data transmission rates, presenting huge growth
opportunities for whitebox Ethernet switches.
Switch ODM-direct vs. server ODM-direct spending growth
CSP General server shipment trend and CAGR
Source: Gartner®.Source: Gartner®, J.P. Morgan estimates.

### Page 29

PCs: Unit downside in sight, but margin/value better than feared

### Page 30

Better-than-feared PC demand in 1H26 likely driven by pull-in orders, cautious on 2H26
PC demand
 1H26 PC shipments appear better-than-expected, likely due to continued Win10 replacement demand and the pull-in of orders ahead of the memory
price hikes.
 We are cautious on PC end demand in 2H26 due to pull-forward demand in 1H26, the negative price elasticity from key component cost hikes, and
late cycle of Win10 PC replacement.
 We anticipate a sub-seasonal shipment trend in 2H26 and forecast a 3% HoH decline for PC shipments.

 We forecast an 8% PC shipment decline this year, comprising 14%/4% consumer/commercial PC shipment decline.
Source: Gartner®, J.P. Morgan estimates.

### Page 32

Unit downside in sight, but PC brands’ margin/value better than feared
 Asian PC brands delivered better-than-expected PC margins in 1H26, driven by better-than-feared PC demand, continued product price hikes, and
product mix adjustments.
 Our research indicates 20-30% YoY growth of PC ASP in 1H26. This, along with the lower-cost PC inventory at hand, drove meaningful margin
expansion for brands.
 In contrast, we saw PC ODM margin dilution in 1H26 due to the ASP hikes, while profit dollars remain intact.

 Looking ahead, while we have seen weakening PC demand in 2H26, we expect PC brands margin trend likely to be better than feared due to
continued product mix adjustments with pricing actions.
 We expect tier one PC brands’ margins to be more resilient in 2H26 due to better supply chain capability.
Source: Gartner®, J.P. Morgan estimates.
Blended PC ASP and YoY trend
Source: Company data. J.P. Morgan estimates. Acer’s 2Q-4Q26 data is based on Bloomberg

consensus estimates.
PC brands PC OPM trend

### Page 33

NB BoM analysis supports a 20-30% PC product price hikes by brands in 1H26
 We estimate that the BoM cost of high-end NBs has increased by ~30% over the past year, mainly driven by the price hikes in memory, non-key
semis (analog ICs), software license fees, and other components (e.g. passive components, PCBs).
 We estimate memory cost to increase by US$200-250 after 3-5x DRAM/NAND price hikes over the past year, driving the memory as % of total

NB BoM to rise to 20-30% vs. HSD % a year ago.
 We also assume a 5% price hike for other components & non-key semis.
 The BoM cost increases have also driven PC brands to implement 20–30% price hikes in 1H26 to protect margins
 We believe mid to low range PCs could have a higher sensitivity to the memory BoM % increase given the lower PC price band.
 PC brands have mitigated the impact through spec downgrades and product mix adjustments.
Source: Gartner®, J.P. Morgan estimates.

NB BoM analysis

### Page 34

Graphics/Motherboards – Soft end demand in the absence of new product ramps
 We have seen weakening end demand for PC motherboard and VGA in 1H26 due to memory supply shortage and negative price elasticity from
product price hikes.
 Looking ahead, we expect a sub-seasonal shipment growth (~5% QoQ growth) for the top 3 MB/VGA vendor shipments in 3Q26 and continue to
forecast a double-digit shipment decline for the full year MB/VGA shipments.
 We see limited catalysts for the market given the lack of new product launches in the next six months.

 This, coupled with the memory-led end demand weakness, implies potential margin pressure for PC component brands.
Source: J.P. Morgan estimates, Company data.
Aggregated Top 3 VGA vendor QoQ trend
Source: J.P. Morgan estimates, Company data.
Aggregated Top 3 MB vendor QoQ trend

### Page 35

Individual stock one-pager – Albert Hung

### Page 36

Delta: Key beneficiary of secular AI datacenter power and thermal trend
 We believe Delta will be a major beneficiary of the fast-growing datacenter power supply TAM (JPMe +80% CAGR in 2025-28), driven by
 Rising power consumption of AI servers
 Non-PSU content increase from power rack adoption and HVDC material upgrades
 Delta possesses the leading position (40%+) in datacenter power (esp. in high-end market) and liquid cooling space.
 We model above-seasonal revenue growth in 3Q/4Q at +16%/+14% QoQ respectively, driven by order push-out from 2Q26, new AI ASIC project

ramp in 2H, 110kW power shelf / HVDC product ramp in 4Q, and product price hikes in late 3Q26.
 We anticipate a gradual margin recovery helped by product price hikes and favorable pricing terms for new products.
 We expect the stock to regain momentum over the next three months as datacenter related revenues re-accelerate. Stay OW.
Source: Company data, J.P. Morgan estimates.
Delta’s earnings and yoy trend
Source: Company data, J.P. Morgan estimates.

Delta’s AI server revenue by product and total OP contribution

### Page 37

ASPEED: Supply unlock in 2H26 drives revenue upsides
 We have seen potential revenue upside near term, driven by robust general server demand, new AI server product cycles, and better supply in 2H26.
 We forecast 19%/33% QoQ revenue growth in 3Q26/4Q26, and early feedback suggests momentum could extend into 1H27.
 We estimate ~38% shipment CAGR for ASPEED’s regular BMC over 2025–28, broadly in line with our server CPU shipment growth forecast.

 ASPEED’s BMC shipments should outgrow CPU volumes due to higher exposure to U.S. CSPs, market share gains, and expanding applications.
 We expect AST2700 penetration to accelerate in 2027 (JPMe ~25%/50% in 27/28). From the meaningful ASP premium for AST2700, the ramp
should be a clear tailwind for ASPEED’s top-line growth.
 ASPEED remains a key beneficiary of the secular server CPU upcycle in our coverage. Stay OW.
Source: Company data, J.P. Morgan estimates.

### Page 38

Lenovo: ISG margin inflection confirmed; AI pipeline doubles
 Lenovo's AI server pipeline more than doubled to US$54bn exiting Jun-Q (vs. US$21bn last Q). Management expects meaningful revenue
conversion in 2HFY27.
 We estimate110%/50% YoY ISG revenue growth in FY27/28.
 We expect a significant OPM improvement for ISG business to 9.3% in FY27 from 0.4% in FY26, driven by favorable pricing and OP leverage.
 Beyond FY27, we anticipate a sustained ISG growth momentum on the multi-year server cycle with continued margin improvement on better

scale.
 IDG profitability also appears better than feared, as Lenovo has been able to outgrow its peers through better supply chain management and scale.
 Looking ahead, we expect continued growth in 2QFY27 as strong ISG/SSG momentum more than offsets seasonal IDG softness.
 We expect ISG to become a key earnings driver over the medium term. IDG profitability also appears better than feared. Stay OW

### Page 39

Accton: Riding AI-driven networking upgrade and unlocking new TAM in server racks
 We forecast Accton to outgrow the whitebox switch market, with a 2025–28  datacenter switch revenue CAGR of ~92% versus an industry TAM
CAGR of ~75%, supported by
 Better-positioned in high-end datacenter switch market (400G+)
 Consolidated switch ODM market & Accton’s market share rebound in Meta 800G projects
 Amazon and Meta raised capex guidance for this year and the implied 2H26 capex uptick indicates sustained revenue momentum for Accton.

 We believe market expectations on AWS T3 supply chain remain undemanding and anticipate the stock to ride on T3 ramp up in 2H26.
 Beyond 2026, Accton plans to expand its capacity by 30%+ next year, which could help sustain Accton’s  growth momentum in the next 1-2
years.OW.

### Page 40

Wiwynn: Well positioned across GPU, AI ASIC, and general servers
 We expect Wiwynn to outperform on new AI server ramps (both ASIC and GPU projects) and a multi-year general server upcycle.
 There could be a re-rating opportunity on the new U.S. CSP ASIC project ramp.
 We expect GPU server to re-accelerate in 4Q26, supported by the initial ramp of Nvidia/AMD GPU servers.
 Overall, we forecast AI server revenue growth of ~73%/~110% in 2026/27.

 Importantly, our industry research suggests Wiwynn is participating in the bidding process for Google’s TPU v8/v9 projects (TPU trays).
 We model a 26% CAGR in Wiwynn’s general server shipments over 2026–28, supported by inference-driven server demand.
 We expect the stock to continue outperforming over the next 3–6 months on new AI server ramps and a multi-year general server upcycle. Stay OW.
Source: Company data, J.P. Morgan estimates.
Wiwynn’s EPS and yoy trend

Source: Company data. J.P. Morgan estimates.

### Page 41

Lotes: Near-term GM headwind, eyes on strong server demand and AI optionality
 Near-term margin weighed down by raw material price increases, depletion of low-cost inventory, and ramp-up costs for new products.
 We expect stable GMs and a mild OPM rebound in 3Q26, with a more meaningful margin recovery in 1H27 as new product cycles ramp.
 We view general server demand intact and keep our forecast of 20–30% server shipment growth over 2025–28, despite near-term supply uncertainty.

 2027 order outlook remains strong with new platform penetration rates likely to reach 30%+
 Lotes has gained market share in the existing Intel/AMD server platforms due to its competitive pricing strategy.
 Looking ahead, management expects 35-40% m/s in the next-gen Intel/AMD server platforms.
 We believe AI products could bring potential upside to Lotes’ business, driven by SOCAMM / QD ramp and mass production of OE socket in 2H27.

NB ODM capacity breakdown and market landscape

### Page 57

What drives the ASP expansion of high-end datacenter switch?
 We estimate a ~150% ASP increase for 800G switches versus 400G switches, driven by
 2-3x higher Switch ASIC cost (Contributing ~70% of the total ASP increase)
 PCB / CCL Materials upgrade to M8 from M6+, 3-4x CCL content growth
 CPO adoption could also significantly increase the switch BoM and bodes well for ODM margins given the high complexity of
component integration (FAU, OE, shuffle box, etc.).

Switch BoM comparison - 400G vs. 800G bare-metal switch

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

研究部對汎德永業營運看法正向，主要是考量：關稅議題淡化，庫存有
效去化 ， 加上新車上市並啟動交付 ， 預期汎德永業下半年業績將明顯改
善，營運開始復甦。此外，4Q26 開始交付新款 iX3，動能亦會延續至
2027 年；2027 年上半年預計還有全新大改款 X5/iX5、3 Series/i3 將
上市，預期 BMW 的新車效應將引領公司明年營運繼續處在回升趨勢上。
研究部預估汎德永業 2026 年 EPS 15.29 元(-16.5%YOY)，2027 年 EPS 
17.80(+16.4%)。以今後4 個季度 EPS 17.16 為評價基礎，給予16 倍本
益比，目標價 275 元，投資評等為買進。

重點摘要：

1. 提供頂級服務與產品體驗之豪華車經銷商： 
汎德永業主要是經營汽車經銷業務及售後保養維修業務 ， 營運
架構分為 ：(1)銷售BMW、MINI，占營收比重約2/3；(2)銷售
Porsche品牌，占營收比重約1/3。就業務性質區分，汎德永業
的業務分為：(1)汽車銷售(包括新車銷售與中古車銷售)，約
占營收九成；(2)保養維修服務，約占營收 一成。車輛銷售毛
利率近幾年約在7-8%；保養維修服務的毛利率則在45-48%。

2. 上半年業績表現不佳，庫存去化壓抑車輛銷售毛利 ： 
汎德永業2Q26合併營收116.71億元(+30%QOQ，-21%YOY)，稅後
淨利3.39億元(+79%QOQ，-32.5%YOY)，2Q26EPS 4.20元。累計
上半年，汎德永業 1H26 稅後淨利 5.28 億元 (-40.5%YOY) ，
1H26EPS 6.55元 。 汎德永業第2季與上半年獲利呈現同比衰退 ，
主要是受到美國關稅政策的預期心理影響 ， 上半年國內進口車
市場買氣依舊觀望，加上BMW、Porsche品牌推出的新車款較少
所致。

分業務來看，在新車銷售方面，因主力品牌 BMW 與 Porsche 
缺乏重磅新車發表 ， 且 MINI 受原廠生產排程影響導致到車量
減少，令汎德永業1H26新車銷售營收減少31.3%YOY；而中古車
銷售營收則是成長 3.8%YOY ； 維修保養業務營收 小幅 成長
1.1%YOY。毛利率方面，因維修保養業務佔比提升至12.2%，帶
動1H26整體合併毛利率上升至11.63%， 但車輛銷售的毛利率因
積極去化新車庫存而由去年同期的7.5%下滑至 6.9%。

資料來源：統一投顧預估

3. 下半年新車到港加速，BMW、Porsche、MINI銷量顯著回升： 
進入下半年，隨著美國關稅議題逐漸淡化，以及台灣整體經濟
成長動能持續，加上多款新車上市，汎德永業預期之後營運將
顯著改善，下半年營收有望 較去年同期成長約10%。在銷量預
估方面，BMW 品牌受惠於多款新年式車款交付，下半年銷售量
預期較上半年增加近50%；Porsche 品牌在主力休旅車電動版
本上市帶動下，業績預計增長 30%；而 MINI 品牌則隨著缺車
問題改善，到車數量將大幅回升，預期下半年銷量較上半年的
增幅可達 50~60%。

4. 2H26至2027年進入新車密集交付期，BMW為主要成長動能： 
新車規劃方面，汎德永業下半年將迎來多款新年式車款，包括
全新年式BMW iX1、iX2、318i Touring等，將陸續到港並展開
交車 。4Q26至2027年，進入重鎊新車交付爆發期 ， 其中BMW全新
純電平台Neue Klasse 首款量產車型 -iX3- 已獲市場高度期
待，該車款目前已手握超過 2,000 張訂單，將在4Q26啟動交
付。此外，全新旗艦 7系列大改款與i7電車也將在4Q26上市。
Porsche品牌有Cayenne與 Cayenne Coupe的電動版，預計於下
半年在台發表並啟動交車，為品牌注入動能。

展望2027年，BMW的iX3訂單持續交車，能有整年度的貢獻，且
明年上半年還將迎 來主力休旅X5/iX5以及主力轎車3 Series
的大改款與i3電動車型上市。配合台中旗艦店與北市科據點陸
續投入營運 ，汎德永業透過資源整併並強化中古車與售後服務
實力，2027年之營運回升力道仍能正面期待。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

5. 2027年BMW主力車型大改款，新車效應延續營運復甦趨勢： 
受惠強勁的新車陣容陸續上市與交付，研究部預期汎德永業
2H26業績將開始回升， 並預估2026年合併營收464.27億元(-
9.8%YOY)，稅後淨利12.34億元(-16.5%YOY)，預估2026年EPS 
15.29元，其中2H26EPS 8.74元，將好於1H26EPS 6.55元。

明年BMW品牌進入Neue Klasse 與核心SUV新產品週期，在三大
主力車型(iX3、X5/iX5、3 Series/i3)新車效應帶動下，業績
有望進一步改善。 研究部預估汎德永業2027年合併營收502.05
億元(+8.1%YOY)，稅後淨利14.37億元(-16.4%YOY)，預估2027
年EPS 17.80元。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

公司簡介 
主要功率元件大廠皆為客戶：順德成立於
1967 年，以辦公文具業務起家；於 1983
年成立電子事業部門，生產半導體導線架，
於 2010 年引進日本三菱伸銅(mitsubishi-
shindoh)成為策略夥伴，取得半導體特殊
材料之製程，提供相關技術之授權與支援。

主要客戶：Infineon、STM、ON SEMI 、
Vishay、Nexperia 等 
主要競爭對手：界霖、長科*

傅泀翰 warrenf.fu@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

順德 (2351 TT) 
均熱片展望越趨明確

永豐觀點 
毛利率表現優於預期；AI 出貨進度超前；均熱片為潛在新動能。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：3Q26 受惠銅利差有利，AI 比重提升與急單附加費，預估毛利率持續向
上，AI 相關產品營收逐漸放大 ， 預期3Q26 比重即可佔到電子產品裡面的比重 10%
以上，2027 年 AI 營收比重可在 10%的基礎上往上墊，樂觀的話評估單月可望挑
戰 20%的營收比重，此外均熱片的潛在產值貢獻龐大，有利評價表現，預估2026
年/2027 年稅後 EPS 6.99 元/8.53 元，維持買進的評等，目標價由 195 元上修至
255 元(約當 2027 年 EPS*PER30X)。

ESG 評析 
順德企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

主要功率元件大廠皆為客戶 ：順德成立於 1967 年 ， 以辦公文具業務起家 ； 於1983
年成立電子事業部門，生產半導體導線架，於 2010 年引進日本三菱伸銅
(mitsubishi-shindoh)成為策略夥伴，取得半導體特殊材料之製程，提供相關技術
之授權與支援 。 主要客戶群則包括Infineon 、STM、ON SEMI、Vishay、Nexperia
等 IDM(整合元件廠)。 
2Q26 毛利率優於預期：2Q26 毛利率達 20.72%，表現優於預期主因，雖然銅價
利差貢獻相較 1Q26 降低很多，但在漲價效應、良率效率稼動率提高，降本(修繕
/電費/化學品)的努力之下，毛利率仍較 1Q26 提升，2Q26 費用達 3.2 億元，其中
包含了一次性的 5000 萬費用，稅後 EPS 1.54 元(原先預估 1.34 元)，表現優於預
期。 
評估 3Q26 毛利率持續向上：展望 3Q26 AI 相關需求強勁，AI 相關產品的營收比
重在 7 月已顯著上升，預期 3Q26 電子產品裡，AI 相關營收比重有望挑戰雙位數
以上的營收佔比，車用市場亦明顯復甦，因此3Q26 將有急單的額外附加費貢獻，
另外銅價利差也有利，而 AI 相關則屬於高毛率的產品線，研究部預估 3Q26 營收
39.8 億元(+11.7%QoQ，+56%YoY)，毛利率 22.47%，稅後 EPS 2.22 元。 
AI 相關需求持續強勁：AI 相關的在手項目已由原本的 40 個提升至 60 個品項；相
關產品包括： PSU、BBU、主板正反面導線架、 800V HVDC 、Power Stage 與
iQFN(低伏特) 等應用，AI 導線架產品以 CLIP 與 To 為大宗(主要用於需要高散熱
的產品)，CLIP 毛利率優於平均，AI 相關佔電子營收的比重從去年的 1% 大幅成
長至 1H26 的 6%，預期在 3Q26 有機會達到雙位數佔比，倘若 60 個品項全部
發酵，評估單月有望佔整體營收比重 20%的水準。 
跨入均熱片領域 ， 潛在產值貢獻龐大 ：公司擁有沖壓 、 金屬加工與電鍍等製程能力 ，
順勢投入開發均熱片產品， 均熱片生產主要有沖壓或是鍛造的方式， 沖壓方式以消
費性產品為主， 順德導線架共平面度可達 20-30 微米 ， 而均熱片客戶僅需 70-80 
微米即可，順德已是四家客戶(包含 IC 設計公司與封測廠)的合格供應商，評估潛
在需求達單月 2,000 萬片；為滿足客戶強勁需求，順德將有積極的擴產計畫，未
來不排除有籌資需求，初步預計在半年內將均熱片產能先擴充至單月  100 萬到 
200 萬片，均熱片市場應用包括：CPU/GPU 均熱片、光通訊均熱片，以及車用與
ASIC 等其他應用，目前發展重心主要在光通訊以及車用、ASIC 等，雖然 2026 年

裕民 (2606 TT)  Buy

產業供需正向發展推升營運動能增溫，投資建議為 Buy。  
投資建議 ：考量產業供需正向發展，推升營運動能增溫，投資
建議為 Buy ，目標價 75.2 元。  
公司船隊 ：公司 1H26 旗下船隊包括 2 艘超大型礦砂輪、 23 艘
海岬型船舶、 24 艘巴拿馬型船舶、 8 艘 Ultramax 船舶，其餘 14
艘則為水泥船及油輪等。 2026 年有一艘與日商合資的 LNG 輪
交 船，此艘LNG 輪持股比例為五成 ， 主要於業外認列獲利貢獻 。 
BCI 及 BPI 指數維持高檔震盪 ： 海岬型船舶方面 ，BCI 指數 2Q26
漲勢明顯， 3Q26 維持高檔震盪，運價走高主要受惠於需求穩定
增長、船舶運力維持低位增長所致；隨西非 Simandou 鐵礦產能
陸續開出，長距航程比重的增加促使市場進一步去化運力。巴
拿馬型船舶方面， 2Q26 因美伊戰事導致國際油價上漲，進而刺
激煤炭需求的替代效應，加上南美洲穀物出口需求增溫，支撐
散裝市場中小型船舶運價走高。近期因需求力道放緩，運價走
勢略有回跌，隨 4Q26 進入傳統北美穀物出口旺季、加上冬季用
電帶動煤炭需求回升，有利支撐船舶運價重拾漲勢；此外，倘
若巴拿馬運河面臨枯水期導致通行艘數嚴重限縮，則巴拿馬型
散裝船恐採取繞道方式，有望進一步推升運價走高。  
預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 7.62 元： 裕民 1Q26 稅後 EPS 1.17 元。
2Q26 受惠於海岬型及巴拿馬型船舶運價同步走揚，加上業外金
融資產評價利益挹注之下 ， 稅後獲利 19.76 億元 ，YoY+204.44% ，
稅後 EPS 2.34 元。3Q26 運價維持高檔震盪，加計業外股利收入
貢獻下，預估稅後獲利 17.69 億元， YoY+21.79% ，稅後 EPS 2.09
元。 4Q26 進入傳統需求旺季，運價有望進一步走高，帶動本業
獲利持續成長，預估稅後獲利 17.07 億元， YoY+30.13% ，稅後
EPS 2.02 元。整體而言，預估裕民 2026 年稅後獲利 64.4 億元，
YoY+76.89% ，稅後 EPS 7.62 元。展望 2027 年，海岬型船舶市
場供需結構仍相對較佳，公司新船下水亦有望對營運帶來正向
貢獻，預估稅後獲利 66.68 億元，稅後 EPS 7.89 元。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 24 日

2 群益投顧 
  公司 ESG 政策：  
裕民由高階主管每年定期辨識氣候相關風險與機會，辨識出之氣候重大風險
與機會如下：  
#轉型風險  
1. 碳強度指標 (CII) 規定  
2. 國際海事組織  2030/2040/2050 淨零查核點  
3. 投資發展低碳船舶  
-業務  
裕民現有船舶須遵守 CII 規定， CII 評等 E 或連續三年為 D 之船舶須提交改善
計畫。為因應 IMO 設定航運產業淨零排放，裕民需藉由發展低碳船舶等方式
逐步減少溫室氣體排放量至淨零碳排放。  
-策略  
裕民透過汰舊換新船舶、進行船舶維護及保養、營運操控等方式減少不必要的
排放。而為邁向淨零，裕民發布淨零路徑圖，同時持續掌握低碳 /零碳船舶研
發、低碳燃料供應情形。  
-財務  
裕民每年花費 22.81 萬美元進行船舶維護保養、預計花費 39 萬美元 (已攤提費
用計算 )安裝轉子帆。另一方面，裕民已有 4 艘較傳統重油船貴 30% 之液化天
然氣雙燃料散裝船投入營運，並於 2023 年投入超過 8 萬美元訓練船員操作新
型化天然氣雙燃料船。  
#氣候相關機會  
1. 以低碳能源，拓展轉型運輸業務  
2. 使用新型燃料降低碳排放，減少碳費支出與罰金  
-業務  
裕民積極布局低碳轉型，搶先同業拓展低碳業務，如建造低碳雙燃料船舶，預
計可於散裝航運市場取得競爭優勢，同時創造良好企業形象，吸引正向投 （融）
資機會，並在減少溫室氣體排放的同時減少碳費支出與罰金。  
-策略  
評估市場低碳運輸需求、低碳燃料供應來源與穩定性、掌握低碳燃料價格變動
趨勢。  
-財務  
裕民投入人力評估低碳燃料供應來源與穩定性及掌握燃料價格變動趨勢、低
碳運輸需求，每年投入約 7.2 萬美元人力資源成本。

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 24 日

3 群益投顧 
公司船隊：  
公司 1H26 旗下船隊共 71 艘，包括 2 艘超大型礦砂輪、 23 艘海岬型船舶、 24
艘巴拿馬型船舶、 8 艘 Ultramax 船舶，其餘 14 艘則為水泥船及油輪等。 2026
年將有一艘與日商合資的 LNG 輪交船，此艘 LNG 輪持股比例為五成，主要於
業外認列獲利貢獻。 2027 年預計交付 2 艘海岬型船、 3 艘 Ultramax 型船及 1
艘離岸風電運輸船。  
2Q26 以來 BCI 及 BPI 指數維持高檔震盪：  
海岬型船舶方面， BCI 指數 2Q26 漲勢明顯， 3Q26 維持高檔震盪，運價走高主
要受惠於鋁礬土及煤炭等需求穩定增長、加上船舶運力維持低位增長所致。隨
西非 Simandou 鐵礦產能陸續開出，長距航程比重的增加促使市場進一步去化
運力。  
巴拿馬型船舶方面， 2Q26 因美伊戰事導致國際油價上漲，進而刺激煤炭需求
的替代效應，加上南美洲穀物出口需求增溫，支撐散裝市場中小型船舶運價走
高。近期因需求力道放緩，運價走勢略有回跌，隨 4Q26 進入傳統北美穀物出
口旺季、加上冬季用電帶動煤炭需求回升，有利支撐船舶運價重拾漲勢；此外，
倘若巴拿馬運河面臨枯水期導致通行艘數嚴重限縮，則巴拿馬型散裝船恐採
取繞道方式，有望進一步推升運價走高。  
圖一、 BCI 及 BPI 指數走勢  
資料來源：群益投顧彙整  
預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 7.62 元：  
裕民 2025 年稅後獲利 36.4 億元， YoY-22.24% ，稅後 EPS 4.31 元。  
1Q26 巴西及澳洲降雨較少，加上散裝市場近年來新船運力增速逐年放緩、供
給結構趨於健康，運價表現淡季不淡；裕民 1Q26 營收 YoY+13.3% ，毛利率
31.96% ，稅後獲利 9.88 億元，稅後 EPS 1.17 元。

4 群益投顧 
2Q26 受惠於海岬型及巴拿馬型船舶運價同步走揚，營收 YoY+38.54% ，毛利
率 35.4% ，加上業外金融資產評價利益挹注之下，稅後獲利 19.76 億元，
YoY+204.44% ，稅後 EPS 2.34 元。  
3Q26 運價維持高檔震盪，加計業外股利收入貢獻下，預估稅後獲利 17.69 億
元， YoY+21.79% ，稅後 EPS 2.09 元。  
4Q26 進入傳統需求旺季，運價有望進一步走高，帶動本業獲利持續成長，預
估稅後獲利 17.07 億元， YoY+30.13% ，稅後 EPS 2.02 元。  
整體而言，預估裕民 2026 年稅後獲利 64.4 億元， YoY+76.89% ，稅後 EPS 7.62
元。  
展望 2027 年，海岬型船舶市場供需結構仍相對較佳，公司新船下水亦有望對
營運帶來正向貢獻，預估稅後獲利 66.68 億元，稅後 EPS 7.89 元。  
投資建議：   
投資建議方面，考量產業供需正向發展，推升營運動能增溫，投資建議為 Buy，
目標價 75.2 元。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

2Q26 beat: ASUS’s 2Q26 revenue was up 37% YoY / 24% QoQ, or 9% / 7% higher 
than our estimate / Bloomberg consensus, which we attribute to the PC pull-in and 
AI server shipments growth. GM improved QoQ, higher than our estimate and 
Bloomberg consensus, which management attribute to ASUS’s strong supply chain 
management and AI server shipments ramp up driving manufacturing efficiency. 
2Q26 opex ratio is better than our estimate, reflecting the operational efficiency 
improvement as revenue scales up and leading 2Q26 net income 88% / 75% higher 
than our estimate.

### Page 2

Earnings revision: We factor in ASUS’s 2Q26 result and raise our 2026-28E net incomes 
by 30% / 2% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues and lower opex ratios. 2026-28E revenues 
are raised by 12% / 8% / 8%, mainly on higher contribution from rack-level AI server 
business as global capacity ramps up. We revise up 2026E GM to reflect the 2Q26 GM 
beat, while revise down 2027-28E GM, as the rising rack-level AI server shipments would 
weigh on the GM. 2026-28E opex ratios are revised down to reflect the operational 
efficiency improvement as revenue scales up.

Valuation: We continue to use near term P/E to derive our 12M TP . Our target P/E is 
updated to 15.7x 2027E EPS, (vs. 15.8x previously), still derived from peers’ avg. ratio of 
2027E PE to 2028E NI YoY and OPM. With the updated earnings estimates and target 
P/E, our 12M TP is raised to NT$1,109 (vs. NT$1,082 previously). Maintain Neutral.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Asus 
We are Neutral-rated on ASUS with a 12-month target price of NT$1,109. Our target 
price is based on 15.7x 2027E P/E, which is derived from brand makers’ average ratio of 
2027E P/E to 2028E NI YoY and OPM. We see EPS growth and OPM as major factors for 
the stock’s share performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger / weaker-than-expected PC market 
growth; 2) faster / slower-than-expected AI and gaming PC growth; and 3) faster / 
slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

BizLink

2Q26 results in line; positive on AI & semi 
equipment; trim PO to NT$3,088; Buy

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 3,088 TWD | Price: 2,275 TWD

2Q in line; GM reflects deployment cycle and product mix 
BizLink’s 2Q GM/OPM of 30.7%/17.2% came in roughly in line with BofAe of 30.9%/ 
17.4% and consensus of 30.7%/17.3%, with 3%/1% higher sales than BofAe/consensus. 
We believe 2Q GM/OPM mainly reflects different deployment cycle among HPC clients 
and product-mix change. HPC comprised 42% of 2Q sales (with a higher mix from power 
vs. data), vs. 45% in 1Q (evenly sales split between power and data). Industrial remained 
the second-largest segment at 35% in 2Q, vs. 34% in 1Q. Yet, sales mix from lighter-
margin EA business rose to 12% in 2Q, vs. 8% in 1Q, after consolidating newly acquired 
PAS. With opex control and a lighter tax rate, NP was 4%/3% above BofAe/consensus. 
Positive on AI & semi equip.; BofAe 2H QoQ sales growth 
Mgmt. remains positive on AI and semi equipment, and sees them as two key structural 
growth drivers. For AI, mgmt. highlighted rising power density and data speed with more 
integrated system requirements. For semi equipment, mgmt. cited a higher value-add 
and clear order visibility into 2H27 as the higher-level assembler and system integrator 
from purely component maker. We now expect 3Q/4Q26 sales to grow 18%/10% QoQ 
and the GM to improve to 31.8%/32.8% (vs. 28.8%/30.7% in 1Q/2Q), supported by new 
AI GPU/ASIC platform launches and sustainable demand from semi equipment business.  
Lower PO to NT$3,088 but reiterate Buy 
We lift 2026-28E EPS by 2-4% to reflect in-line 2Q26 results, solid AI momentum, sales 
from Interplex Datacom acquisition from 2027 (see our 11 June note), and subtotal 5% 
share dilution from new GDR and ECB issued in mid-Aug. We trim PO to NT$3,088 (28x 
2027E EPS) from NT$3,230 (30x 2027E EPS), with the valuation base unchanged but a 
lower P/E multiple. We now use 28x (3SD above average) on potentially slower margin 
expansion amid clients’ different deployment schedule. Yet, we reiterate Buy as BizLink 
benefits from AI copper/power/optical cable demand with volume/content increase.

Key Metrics      
* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 8.

Company Sector 
Connectors, Passive Components & Distribution

Company Description 
BizLink is a global interconnect solutions provider, making 
products like cables, wire harnesses connectors, etc. 
BizLink's key business segments are IT DataCom (43% of 
2025 sales), Industrial (34%), Automotive (11%), and 
Electrics Appliance (11%). BizLink is headquartered in the 
Silicon Valley, California, US. Globally, it has 40 production 
sites post INBG acquisition in 2022. It was founded in 1996 
and was listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2011.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to BizLink in view of (1) 
cable/connector volume/content increase under strong AI 
server market demand and rising computing power, (2) 
BizLink's solid delivery in better-margin HPC business, (3) 
solid partnership with Credo for AEC business and 
expansion into optical business, (4) industrial business 
bottoming out, (5) ongoing margin expansion with 
improving mix.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
BizLink (BIZLF) 
Our PO of NT$3,088 is based on 28x 2027E EPS. Our valuation multiple of 28x (3SD 
above historical average since IPO) reflects positive outlook for better-margin HPC 
business to ride on multi-year AI demand, content/volume growth driven by ongoing 
copper/power/optical cable/connector development for AI, industrial business overall 
bottoming out, robust semi equipment demand with over one year order visibility, and 
expanding margins.

Upside risks: (1) stronger demand in AI, (2) stronger semi-cap/datacenter market 
demand, (3) improving profitability for EV business, (4) margin upside from more stable 
raw material prices (copper, plastic, gold) and operating leverage, (5) favorable FX 
movement, and (6) more synergies from M&A.

Downside risks: (1) delayed order from AI business, (2) intensifying competition and/or 
pricing pressure, (3) weaker macro to hinder Industrial market demand, (4) more intense 
competition in EV business, (4) GM pressure from higher raw material prices and/or 
increasing labor cost in China/US/Europe/Malaysia, (5) unfavorable FX movement, and 
(6) less synergies from M&A.

### Page 1

ab 22 August 2026
Global Research
First Read
BizLink Holding
Q226 margin largely in line; we expect robust
H226 growth
Q: How did the results compare vs expectations?
A: BizLink's Q226 operating profit rose 29% QoQ, 6%/2% above the UBS/consensus
estimates, on a gross margin recovery. Net profit rose 31% QoQ, 6%/1% above the
UBS/consensus estimates, on a lower effective tax rate.
Q: What were the most noteworthy areas in the results?
A: Q226 sales increased 12% QoQ, primarily supported by growth in electrical

appliances (EA) (+56% QoQ), industrial (+13% QoQ) and automotive (+12% QoQ). The
EA increase was largely attributable to a sales contribution from PAS, a newly acquired
subsidiary that began to be consolidated into the financial statements in May 2026.
Industrial sales benefited from continued momentum in SPE (+28% QoQ), as the box-
build business continued to strengthen. IT and datacom sales rose only 2% QoQ, as
shipments were slow due to a product transition during April-May. BizLink's monthly

sales picked up strongly in June-July. We view this as evidence of HPC product shipments
returning to a normal pattern. Gross margin expanded 2.0ppt QoQ, above the UBS
estimate. That was mainly supported by higher utilization, effective material cost
management and production efficiency improvement. However, it was partially offset by
a higher-than-expected opex/sales ratio, as scale benefits were less than we expected.
Q: Has the company's outlook/guidance changed?

A: BizLink did not provide quantitative guidance, while remaining constructive on
sustained order growth driven by the AI investment cycle, even though data center
deployment  is not linear. BizLink indicated it is selective in capacity expansion, with two
major criteria: customer-backed demand and decent ROI.   XFS has expanded capacity in
fiber assembly to support robust growth despite still accounting for a small part of the
sales mix. The Interplex deal is scheduled to be completed before year-end 2026

(schedule unchanged). Aside from HPC, management is positive about capital
equipment (box-building services for semi equipment customers) and automation.
How would we expect investors to react?
A: We think the results/earnings call is a neutral event. We expect sustained strong
earnings growth of 28% QoQ in Q326 to serve as a positive share price catalyst,
supported by data cable shipments returning to a normal pattern, cable shipments for a
new ASIC platform and a gross margin recovery, as utilization keeps increasing. There

may be upside from power cables, as power sidecars could be adopted earlier. We raise
our 2026-28E earnings 5-7% to 44%/83%/22% growth, mainly to factor in UBS latest
view on rack shipments and ASIC shipments volumes, along with higher opex/sales. We
raise our PT from NT$3,430 to NT$3,617 (same 30x 2027E PE). We remain at Buy.

Company Description
Founded in 1996, BizLink is headquartered in Fremont, California. BizLink provides
interconnect solutions, including connectors, harnesses and cables for a wide range of
industries, such as industrials, automotive, IT & data communications, and electrical
appliances. BizLink has 34 production facilities located in Asia, America and Europe.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on a PE multiple.
The key company-specific risks are: 1) a global economic slowdown impacting end-demand;
2) a slower-than-expected change in the product mix leading to slower-than-expected gross-
margin expansion; and 3) a rise in raw material prices.
The key industry-specific risks are: unexpected changes in the underlying global macro
environment, currency and interest rate movement and commodity price inflation.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Taiwan Electronic Components
28 August 2026 Citi Research
New opportunity in Lens-Based Optical Connectivity
The rapid growth of AI infrastructure is changing the role of optics inside data
centers. In the past, optical fiber was mainly used to connect switches over
relatively long distances, while electrical copper connections handled shorter links.
AI changes this balance because thousands of GPUs must communicate with one
another at extremely high speed. As network speeds move from 400G and 800G

toward 1.6T, 3.2T and beyond, the industry is moving from traditional pluggable
optical modules toward linear optics, near-packaged optics and eventually co-
packaged optics (CPO), where the optical engine moves much closer to the
processor or switch chip.
We see Largan and Genius are targeting different potential markets in AI optics.
Largan has been working on Fiber Array and PMLA while Genius is more at optical
connector and also micro lens. We see great potential on both companies and

expect the contribution will start in 1H27 for Genius at earliest and 2H27 for
Largan.
Largan- Fiber Array + PALM in good progress
During its latest earnings call in July, Largan has elaborated technology and
opportunity in CPO (see our report of Largan Precision (3008.TW) - Phone camera
upgrade + Fiber Array upside), in particular in the fiber array potential. Thanks to its
better optics precision and alignment technology, Largan seems to be confident

about its Fiber array opportunity, and the pilot run production line will be ready in
3Q26 with mass volume production in 2H27 at the earliest, earlier than our
previous estimate in 2028.
Looking forward, Largan’s multi-layer Fiber Array architecture represents a shift
from traditional linear fiber arrangements toward a planar optical interface capable
of substantially increasing IO density. This architecture directly addresses one of
the key bottlenecks in future CPO and silicon photonics systems, where optical

throughput growth increasingly depends on higher IO density and more precise
fiber-to-chip alignment rather than solely faster SerDes speeds.
Largan already possesses extensive expertise in precision optics, including lens
molding, optical coatings, automated production, and in-house equipment
development. As a result, manufacturing capacity expansion may be less
challenging than technology qualification. The likely path involves customer
validation and engineering qualification first, followed by pilot production and

eventual volume ramp. We believe customer adoption proceeds smoothly,
commercialization could begin with limited production before scaling into larger
deployments. Strategically, the opportunity extends beyond supplying Fiber Arrays
alone. By integrating PMLA, micro-lens technologies, and advanced optical
interfaces, Largan could position itself higher in the optical interconnect value
chain.
We estimate Largan’s optical revenue for AI data center would contribute

c10%/c20% in 2027 /2028 assuming overall 30m and 50m FAU demand and
Largan’s share of 10% and 25%.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Source: Citi Research,  Largan Source: Citi Research,  Largan
Genius – Lens-base connector for Fiber
With AI data center, connecting an enormous number of fibers reliably has been a

rising challenge. Traditional multi-fiber connectors depend on physical contact,
meaning that very small fiber cores must meet precisely. Dust, scratches or poor
cleaning can therefore cause signal loss and installation failures. Genius is
targeting leading optical solution company with tiny lens in front of each fiber to
expand the light beam before it crosses the connector and the receiving fiber.
This “expanded-beam” approach makes the connection less sensitive to small

particles and physical wear. The lens therefore becomes more than a simple optical
component: it may become an important interface between increasingly
complex AI systems and the huge fiber networks connecting them.
Thanks to its large scale in automated lens manufacturing, assembly, inspection
and testing experience, we expect Genius’ lens connector for fiber would start
shipment in 1H27. Its solution will combine the ferrule with lens reflection

collimation technology to expand the light path and overcome the effect of
surface particles on insertion loss and return loss performance. Note that
Genius’ experience in coating and big-scale manufacturing will enable the
company to secure great potential in this new opportunity.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 5

Taiwan Electronic Components
28 August 2026 Citi Research
Figure 3. Genius - optical connector and ferrule solution
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Citi Research, Company Reports
Smartphone lens- benefit from new iPhone lens upgrade
With Apple’s lens upgrade for variable apertures and potentially machinal shutter
next year, we see stable ASP trend with upside potential for lens module makers,

including Largan and Genius. T oday, smartphone camera lenses primarily consist of
multiple high-precision plastic aspherical lenses assembled into a compact lens
barrel, while autofocus and optical image stabilization are handled by separate
actuators. Adding a mechanical shutter would introduce another moving
component into this tightly constrained optical path, increasing requirements for
lens alignment, mechanical tolerance, space management, and coordination

between the lens, shutter, actuator, and CMOS image sensor. The more important
implication would emerge if mechanical shutters develop alongside variable-
aperture systems, where the physical aperture can change according to lighting
and photographic conditions. This could materially increase the design and
manufacturing barriers for high-end plastic lenses, particularly as tolerances for
lens decenter, tilt, aperture positioning, and sensor alignment become increasingly

interconnected. Therefore, the longer-term opportunity for leading lens suppliers is
not simply additional lens elements or higher P-count, but greater integration of
optics and precision mechanics. We see the trend would benefit Largan most, as
we believe Largan will focus more on the most advanced lens design for variable
aperture+ machinal shutter, and there is great chance that Genius may also gain
more share in ultra-wide angle and wide angle lens.

We estimate that Largan has the majority share of current iPhone’s focal lens and
more than 50% share in the wide angle and 30% of the ultra-wide angle lens.
Genius may gradually take some share in focal lens and keep 20-30% share in wide
angel and ultra-wide in our view.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Largan - Maintain Buy and lift TP to NT$8,500
We raise our 2027 /28 earnings forecast for Largan by 5%/25% as we factor in the
better than expected development in its Fiber Array+PMLA business and its
aggressive capacity expansion target as it just announced another land expansion
plan in view of the solid outlook. We raise our TP to NT$8,500 (from NT$6,125)
based on 30x our 2027 /2028E EPS. We lift our PER target to 30x from 25x on

better order visibility, which is proven by its recent capacity expansion plan. 30x
PER is higher than its 5-year historical range as we expect the promising growth
opportunity from its new CPO business should drive a further re-rating for the
stock.

Genius – Upgrade to Buy and lift TP to NT$1,050
We raise our 2026/27 earnings forecast for Genius by 8%/14% as we factor in its
smooth progress in optical connector and ferrule business and resilient lens
revenue from Apple and share gains potential in focal lens space. We also introduce

our 2028 numbers in this note. Given new business opportunity in optical
connectors and solid sales outlook from existing lens business, we upgrade Genius
from Sell to Buy. We roll over our TP valuation base year from previous 2026 EPS to
now 2027 /28 average as we view the new business in optical connector as a new
catalyst starting from next year. Also, we lift our target PER to now 25x from
previous 13x as we believe the high growth optical opportunity in AI arena will lead

to better growth and re-rating. Genius used to trade based on the smartphone
product cycle and the growth has been stagnant. With the new optical connector,
it should be resuming strong growth. 25x is in line with its historical peak-cycle
PER. Our TP is thus raised to NT$1,050 based on 25x our 2027 /2028E EPS.

Genius Electronic Optical
Company description
Genius is a leading lens provider. It was established in 1989 and listed on
TWSE in 2005. Its revenue increased from NT$4.5bn in 2010 to NT$23.2bn in
2024. The revenue breakdown for Genius in 2023 was 73% smartphone lens
and 27% others.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Genius. We expect Genius to see resilient sales
momentum driven by stable lens sales in iPhone business. Moreover, we see

its new development in optical connector and ferrule to provide strong upside
potential in the longer term.
Valuation
Our target price for Genius of NT$1,050 is based on 25x 2027 /28 EPS. We are
using 2027 /28 as teh valuation base year as we believe the new business in
optical connector as a new catalyst starting from next year. 25x PER is at the
high end of its 5-year historical range as we believe the high growth optical
opportunity in AI arena will lead better growth and re-rating. Genius used to

trade based on smartphone product cycle and the growth has been stagnant.
With the new optical connector, it should be resuming strong growth.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean the stock fails to achieve our target price
include: lower-than-expected smartphone sell-through; lower iPhone share
allocation; slower-than-expected spec migration for the lens industry; and
slower than expected prorgess in new optical business.
Largan Precision
Company description

Largan is a plastic/glass/hybrid lens supplier in Taiwan with end-applications
including phone cameras, DSCs, MFPs, and tablets. More than 80% of its
revenue is driven by phone camera lenses.
Investment strategy
We rate Largan a Buy as we expect its core iPhone business to remain resilient
and its co-packaged optics (CPO) development to provide strong upside
potential in the longer term. We believe Largan could be a long-term winner in
the lens market driven by: 1) its technology leadership; 2) intellectual property

(IP) protection; and 3) higher yield vs. competitors. In the longer term, we
believe spec migration could keep Largan ahead of competitors.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$8,500 for Largan shares is based on 30x 2027 /28E
EPS. We are using 2027 /28E EPS average as we see new Fiber Array + PMLA
revenue to kick in from next year. Our 30x PER is higher than its historical 5-
Prepared for Kevin Lu

15
year PER range, which we think is justified by its better order visibility proven
by its recent strong capacity expansion plan, and we expect its CPO business
engagement should provide a strong long-term growth opportunity.
Risks
Key downside risks include: 1) weaker smartphone demand or replacement
demand, and in particular, demand headwinds for certain key clients; 2)
longer learning curve and slower ramp of the yield rate; 3) slower spec

migration; and 4) slower development in CPO business. Any of these risk
factors could cause the shares to deviate from our target price.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

We think Nvidia’s recent guidance for 70% revenue growth in
CY27 should be positive for members of Taiwan's supply chain
with higher related revenue exposure.
Please see Joe Moore’s take on Nvidia’s recent results:  NVIDIA very positive 2027

commentary (27 Aug 2026). Among other highlights, 70%+ CY27 revenue growth
guidance is a remarkable figure, and to the extent possible we would expect Nvidia
to continue to knock down barriers to higher growth. Management framed the
demand as "much greater" than that 70% level. That echoes our  previous 2027
CoWoS forecast in  Asia-Pacific Technology: AI Supply Chain: Preliminary 2027 TSMC
CoWoS Allocation (23 Jun 2026): Nvidia uses TSMC’s CoWoS-L as the single source

for its AI GPU products (e.g., Blackwell and Rubin). Its 2027 CoWoS-L consumption
could reach ~910k, up ~40% Y/Y. Strong CoWoS-R bookings by Nvidia also suggest
very strong Vera CPU shipments (almost doubling). Factoring in the chip price hike
starting in 1Q next year, 70%+ revenue growth looks consistent with TSMC CoWoS
implied chip shipments.
Is that a strong catalyst for Nvidia’s supply chain in Taiwan? We think yes: We
cite the strong growth outlook, ongoing tightness, and cheap valuation:

• We still think TSMC’s wafer front-end (3nm) and ABF substrate supply
remain major constraints, as Nvidia goes through HBM spec optimization
(see Asia-Pacific Technology: AI Supply Chain: GPU Architecture Optimization
Given Memory Shortage and Supernode Challenge (10 Aug 2026) ). We
expect TSMC to succeed with a wafer price hike  in 2027 – we view a 5%-10%
hike as likely.
• KYEC’s 3Q26 revenue suffered from wafer constraints. With Rubin ramping
on schedule, we expect strong sequential growth in 4Q26 for KYEC’s GPU

and CPU revenue.
•  See Exhibit 2  for Nvidia revenue exposure across semiconductor and tech
hardware. Most stocks’ 2027e PEG still screens below 0.7 on average.

Aligning Blackwell and Rubin chip shipments with HGX and rack shipments
We analyzed Blackwell chip shipments versus rack shipments in our  last  June AI tracker.
We believe TSMC's Blackwell chip output, based on CoWoS-L capacity, aligns more closely

with underlying demand as reflected in AI capex trends.
We now incorporate HGX systems (8 GPUs per server) into our chip consumption model
and assume that nine HGX servers are equivalent to one NVL72 rack. We forecast 5.4mn
Blackwell units in 2026, with chip supply sufficient to meet Grace Blackwell NVL72
demand by 2H26.
Our latest checks also suggest shipments of nearly 7mn Rubin and Rubin Ultra units in
2027, while total Rubin NVL72 rack shipments could reach 90k units in 2027 (Exhibit 9 ).

All Blackwell chip "inventory" has proven to be supply-chain buffer stock and should be
fully absorbed by 2026. As a result, we see little reason for concern over inventory levels.
We expect Rubin to follow a similar pattern. Rubin should begin ramping up in 3Q26, with
rack shipments starting in 4Q26.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Unimicron
Valuation
Our target price for Unimicron is NT$1,500. We use a 1-year forward-looking DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect
Unimicron's ability to generate stable cash. With a risk-free rate of 4.3% (10-year government bond rate), a market risk
premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 1.43 (2-year weekly share price), we calculate Unimicron's WACC as 13.7%. Our target
price is equivalent to 66x/31x 2026/27E EPS, which is higher than the +1std level of consensus PE multiple of the past five

years. However, as the market focuses on 2027 upcycle, we believe valuation on 2027E EPS is still in line with historical peak
valuations of 25–30x during the last upcycles.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean Unimicron shares trade below our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected HDI
business; 2) significant increase in ABF supply in the industry; and 3) stronger-than-expected margin pressure from
depreciation due to higher capex investments.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

### Page 5

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

高教機交付進入尾聲，惟評價已達合理區間 
 高教機交付進入尾聲，民用業務成長，預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 0.57 元：
展望 3Q26，高教機預計於 2H26 完成交付；民用業務方面，兩大飛機
製造商在手訂單持續暢旺，合計仍需逾 10 年消化，有望帶動漢翔發動
機業務成長，預估 3Q26 營收 98.45 億元(+7.1%QoQ)。獲利方面，受
到原物料成本上漲影響，漢翔已依個別專案逐步反映成本，惟價格調
整仍存在時間差，且高教機相關罰款預計持續認列，預估稅後淨利 5.4
億元(-44.1%QoQ)，3Q26 稅後 EPS 0.57 元。 
 民用與科服需求延續 ， 預估2026 年稅後 EPS 2.47 元：展望 2026 年，
國防部分，高教機專案 結束後，後續成長動能將轉向軍機維保及無人
機業務；民用受惠全球航空市場需求維持強勁， 科服相關專案皆依計
畫執行中。預估 2026 年營收 379.9 億元(+7.2%Y oY)，隨高教機罰款
影響趨緩，預估稅後淨利 23.3 億元(+217.2%Y oY)，2026 年稅後 EPS 
2.47 元。展望 2027 年，民用業務需求仍維持暢旺，且科服專案認列
比重可望提升，預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 2.13 元(尚未考量無人機專案)。 
 目標價 75 元，維持中立評等：評價方面，以 2Q27F BVPS 計算目前
PB 3.5X， 介於漢翔近十年 PB 1.8-3.6X 中緣 ， 考量評價已達合理區間 ，
且無人機預算審議中， 維持中立 ， 目標價75 元(2Q27F BVPS PB 3.6X)。

漢翔為我國航太龍頭廠  漢翔成立於 1996 年 7 月 ， 實收資本額94.19 億元 ， 截至1Q26 底 ， 員工共5,110
人 。 主要從事軍用航空(包含國內軍機製造維修 、機隊商維 、 軍用引擎業務等)、
民用航空(包含民用飛機機體結構 、 次組合設計加工及國際民用飛機引擎零組件
設計加工及代工製造等 )與工業技術服務(包含創能、儲能、資訊服務及售後服
務等)等業務。1H26 應用別為國防 44%，民用 54%，科技服務 2%。

高教機交付進入尾聲 ，民用業務
成長，預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 0.57
元 
 展望 3Q26，高教機預計於 2H26 完成交付；民用業務方面，兩大飛機製造商
在手訂單持續暢旺，合計仍需逾 10 年消化，有望帶動漢翔發動機業務成長，
預估 3Q26 營收 98.45 億元(+7.1%QoQ，+11.7%Y oY)。獲利方面，受到原物
料成本上漲影響，漢翔已依個別專案逐步反映成本，惟價格調整仍存在時間
差，且高教機相關罰款預計持續認列，預估毛利率 10.92%，營益率 7.57%，
假設匯率持穩，預估稅後淨利 5.4 億元(-44.1%QoQ，+179.5%Y oY)，3Q26 稅
後 EPS 0.57 元。

Exhibit 6：漢翔近年罰款走勢

Sources : Company，MasterLink 
國防業務進入轉換期 ，民用與科
服需求延續，預估 2026 年稅後
EPS 2.47 元 
 展望 2026 年，國防部分，高教機專案預計 2H26 全數交付完畢，相關罰款影
響可望逐步趨緩，有助獲利結構改善，惟隨專案結束，營收貢獻將有所縮減 ，
後續成長動能將轉向軍機維保及無人機業務。隨軍機出勤率提升，維保需求可
望持續增加；無人機及無人機反制系統相關標案亦陸續開標，其中Jul-26 漢翔
透過與 Shield AI 合作之 V-BAT 垂直起降無人機 ， 取得海巡署約12 億元艦載無
人機標案，預期將依專案進度挹注營收。海外布局方面，美國廠已於 Jun-26
完成 ISO 9001 認證，目前仍處於試產階段，後續將視客戶驗證及訂單導入進
度逐步放量。

民用業務方面，受惠全球航空市場需求維持強勁，主要飛機製造商訂單能見度
穩定，帶動零組件需求維持穩健。又 Jul-26 漢翔於英國法茵堡航展與多家客戶
簽訂總額逾 220 億元之新單，有助提升中長期訂單能見度，預期民用業務將持
續成為營運成長動能 。科服業務方面，除 2Q25 取得台電「花蓮鳳林變電站
161kV 靜態同步補償器(STATCOM)新建工程案」外，2Q26 亦取得「台電 MW
級混氫型氣渦輪發電機組統包工程建置案」統包工程，目前相關專案皆依計畫
執行中。另，水湳廠區微電網應用案場預計於 2026 年上線，後續可望作為示
範場域，進一步爭取國內用電大戶，帶動電力島及相關能源系統建置需求。

3042 晶技(TXC)

報告日期： 2026/08/24 研 究 員：洪瑞陽  (Ryan Hung) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收 36.9 億元， EPS 1.62 元， 7 月營收創高   
2Q26 營收  36.9 億元， QoQ +10.8% 、 YoY +9.9% 。毛利率  
33.11%， 營益率 16.82% ， 稅後淨利率 15.09% 。 單季稅後純益 5.58
億元， QoQ +24.0% 、YoY +50.8% ，EPS 1.62 元。營運動能延續
至第三季： 7 月自結營收  13.66 億元， MoM +14.48% 、 YoY 
+22.91% ，為 58 個月新高 ， 歷史第四高 ； 累計1~7 月營收  84.04 億
元、歷史次高。公司並公布  7 月自結稅前  EPS 0.86 元，單月獲
利同步走揚，反推毛利率接近 36% ，重回歷史最高獲利狀況
(2021-22 年消費性電子爆發年 36-38%) ，本次由雙 A 帶動，後市
可期。公司於法說簡報預估  2026 年  AI 占比自  2025 年  11% 
升至  16% 、車用自  26% 升至  28% ，行動通訊降至  24% 、消費
性 14% ；公司於法說表示  7 月光模組營收占比已高於上半年，
且下半年毛利率朝  36% 邁進，接單動能延續。

二、 AI＋車用驅動產品組合結構性升級，獲利成長  
資料中心： 2027 年 800G 將往  1.6T 放量。隨不同  CSP 架構各
自演進，單機所需時脈 (clock) 數量增加，並朝更高參考頻率 (目前
156/312 未來  625MHz) 、更低抖動發展；石英低抖動、高  Q 的特
性正對應此需求， TCXO/OCXO 則確保節點同步。就價值而言，
公司於法說表示前十大光模組廠皆為其客戶，公司光模組相關產
品 ASP 隨世代顯著躍升 ，800G 約 1 美元 、1.6T 約 2~5 美元、
3.2T 約 5~7 美元，公司於光模組的石英市占約 20% 
車用：隨汽車電子化與智能化加速， ADAS 與智能座艙帶動感測
需求，用量上， L2 升級至  L3 每台車約增加  45 顆，L3 以上一
台車的頻率元件用量可達  70~80 顆(傳統車僅  20~30 顆)，整車
滲透率持續向上。公司於法說表示，車用主力在智能座艙與駕駛，
在中國市場石英市占已居首，並持續拓展歐系、日系客戶，寧波
亦設有車用專廠。晶技的產品組合正由傳統消費性向「雙  A(AI
＋車用 )」成長引擎結構性升級：公司於法說簡報預估  AI 占比自  
2025 年 11% 升至  2026 年 16%、 車用自 26% 升至  28% ，並 明
確指引 AI＋車用 2027 年合計占比將突破五成。

三、產品升級為系統層級，成本優勢築高護城河  
近期市場關注的  MEMS 是否取代石英，公司認為只有些許部分
影響，是競爭者但不是替代者，石英成本優勢太大。其論點在於：

2 
MEMS 屬半導體製程、僅做設計而其餘委外，定位落在 clock/ 振
盪器範疇，並非能取代全部石英的技術；純被動的石英晶體僅需
兩隻接腳 (2-pin) 、單顆約  4~5 美分， MEMS 因結構上必含  
ASIC ，故 MEMS 應對標  TCXO/XO 一類主動產品而非裸晶體。
就成本結構而言， MEMS 屬半導體，一次  12 吋光罩 (tape-out)
投入動輒約上百萬美元、失敗難以回收，石英的成本優勢就顯現。
公司並指出， MEMS 業者多年虧損、近年才因量體出現而轉盈；
其早期能切入光模組，主要憑藉美系光環、強勢行銷與龐大通路，
惟一旦進入亞洲  OEM 供應鏈，客戶對單一供應商的疑慮將帶來
第二供應商的替代空間，晶技如何於早期切入客戶反為需補強的
課題。整體而言，公司視  MEMS 為競爭者而非取代者。

四、預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 7.17/9.03 元 
預估  2026 年營收  150.8 億元、 YoY +13.0% ，毛利率  34.5% 、
營益率  18.5% ，稅後純益  24.6 億元，EPS 7.17 元。2027 年營收  
164.7 億元、 YoY +9.2% ，毛利率升至  36.4% 、營益率  21.5% ，
稅後純益  31.0 億元， EPS 9.03 元、 YoY +25.9% 。隨雙  A 高毛
利占比提升與費用率因規模擴大而下降，營業利益成長幅度顯著
大於營收 (2026 營益  YoY +38% 、2027 +27%) ， 營益率自 2025 年 
15.2% 逐年攀升至  2027 年 21.5%

五、投資建議：買進，目標價  235 元 
考量雙  A 結構性成長、 AI 光通訊與車用題材延續、獲利彈性優
於營收，給予略高於歷史之 26X 2027 EPS ，目標價  235 元 
貳、公 司 概 況 
一、公司簡介  
台灣晶技為一專業之頻率控制元件供應商，自 1983 年成立以來，
始終致力於石英元件系列產品之研發、設計、生產與銷售，專事
生產高精密、高品質之石英諧振器 (Crystal Units) 、石英振盪器
(Crystal Oscillators) 等頻率元件系列產品，相關產品皆可廣泛使用
於行動通訊、電信、資通訊及儲存運算設備、物聯網 (IoT) 、智慧
家庭、人工智慧、高效能運算卡、醫療、各類型連接技術、汽車
電子、電動車等應用市場。依據發展策略及市場需求，持續投入
研發資源，積極發展新技術，以“微型化、高穩定度、模組化”
為產品發展方針，擴大在高端應用及高附加價值產品之市場佔  
有率 。 晶技是全球石英元件之產品應用布局最廣 、 營業規模最大、
市佔率為最高，於石英產業居領導地位。全球生產據點涵蓋台灣
平鎮 (先進半導體製程、高頻高精度產品主力 )、中國寧波 (車用專
廠與前段晶片小型化 )與重慶、印尼泗水 (非紅供應鏈基地 )及日本

MHz Crystal( 石英晶體 /無源晶體 )最基礎的被動元件，僅由石英晶
片封裝而成。自身無法發振，必須外接主晶片 (SoC/MCU) 的振盪
電路才能運作；精度較低且易受溫飄影響。為成本最低、體積最
小的基準元件，廣泛用於一般消費電子、家電、 PC 主機板及車載
基本控制板。  
MHz Crystal Thermistor(TSX/ 熱敏晶體 )本質仍為被動元件 ， 在晶體
封裝內直接整合一顆熱敏電阻。對比前者多了「溫度感測能力」 ，
能即時將溫度傳給主晶片進行軟體補償，用低成本與微型封裝達
到接近  TCXO 的高精度。多用於智慧型手機、 Wi-Fi 6/7 、藍牙及  
5G-A/6G AIoT 邊緣裝置。  
XO/VCXO( 石英振盪器 /壓控振盪器 )主動元件，將石英晶片與發振  
IC 整合於同一封裝內 ， 通電即可直接輸出時脈 。 對比前者多了「主
動發振  IC( 與壓控引腳 )」， 擺脫外部驅動 ， 並支援差分輸出(sub-30

fs 極低相位抖動 )與電壓微調。主要用於  800G~3.2T AI 光模組、
Super NIC 網卡、 PCIe Gen 6/7 伺服器。  
TCXO( 溫度補償石英振盪器 )高精度主動元件，內部  IC 內建硬體
溫度補償電路，能自動修正頻率偏置。對比前者多了「硬體自動
溫補電路」 ，克服極端溫差下的頻率失真，精準度大幅提升至  
0.1~2 ppm( 甚至  ppb 級)。廣泛應用於  5G/6G 終端與  RAN 、車
載 ADAS/V2X 通訊、 GPS 定位與  AI 伺服器時間同步。  
OCXO( 恆溫晶體振盪器 )石英元件的最高階形態，內部設有微型加
熱爐 (Oven) 將晶片維持於固定高溫 (如 85 ˚C)。多了「微型加熱爐
(物理恆溫 )」，從被動補償升級為主動控制，徹底消除外界溫飄，
達到級精度與斷網守時 (Holdover) 能力。主要用於  6G 通感一體
(ISAC) 與 BBU 基地台 、 電信骨幹網 、AI 與 PTP 時脈同步設備。

二、 AI 與車用驅動，產品組合轉佳帶動公司成長  
晶技由過去消費性轉往 AI 與車用電子布局：  
其一為資料中心 。 光模組迭代速度遠快於過去五年 ，OFC 上 3.2T 
方案已亮相， 2027 年 800G 將往  1.6T 放量。隨不同  CSP 架構
各自演進，單機所需時脈 (clock) 數量增加，並朝更高參考頻率 (如 
625MHz) 、更低抖動發展；石英低抖動、高  Q 的特性正對應此需
求， TCXO/OCXO 則確保節點同步。就價值而言，公司於法說表
示前十大光模組廠皆為其客戶，公司光模組相關產品  ASP 隨世
代顯著躍升， 800G 約 1 美元、1.6T 約 2~5 美元、3.2T 約 5~7 
美元，公司於光模組的石英市占約 20%，1.6T/3.2T 多處於打樣送
樣階段。至於  625MHz 產品，公司表示仍在開發與，屬一至二年
後的產品，尚無客戶量產。  
其二為  6G 。相較過往僅承載資訊傳輸， 6G 新增感測 (sensing) 功
能，客戶規格選擇朝更高頻、更嚴格演進，數量穩定成長之餘，
對單一元件的價值要求亦提升。與之相關的低軌衛星，公司視為  
6G 布局的一環，延續  5G 、且  Amazon 、Tesla 等大舉布建；晶
技長期為主要供應商、送樣跟進緊密，惟目前營收比重仍低，公
司傾向將其歸入 AI 佔比。  
其三為車用。隨汽車電子化與智能化加速， ADAS 與智能座艙帶
動感測需求，車輛  E/E 架構由分散式  ECU 往中央集中運算演
進；用量上， L2 升級至  L3 每台車約增加  45 顆，L3 以上一台
車的頻率元件用量可達  70~80 顆(傳統車僅  20~30 顆)，整車滲
透率持續向上。公司於法說表示，車用主力在智能座艙與駕駛，
在中國市場石英市占已居首，並持續拓展歐系、日系客戶，寧波
亦設有車用專廠。  
其四為邊緣  AI 與機器人。此類應用同樣需要晶體與晶振，並要

單一模組化產品約需  70~80 顆晶體，並需大量感測。  
小型化為長期趨勢， 1008(1.0 ×0.8mm) 為目前業界最主流尺寸，
0806(0.8 ×0.6mm) 則處於導入階段，將隨客戶需求進入量產，趨勢

26% 升至  28% ，並明確指引  AI ＋車用  2027 年合計占比將突破
五成；同時行動通訊主動降至  24% 、消費性降至  14% ，聚焦關鍵
客戶的質量提升。 2026 年資本支出達  10.78 億元，其中約八成
投入  AI 相關產品，桃園廠區並投入  6.9 億元擴充高頻、高精
度、低相位噪聲產品產能，寧波廠擴充前段晶片製程、小型化與
車用產能，印尼廠則對應非紅需求。  
圖四、新產品往高頻、小型化、低抖動、高 Q 值發展

資料來源：公司法說會簡報

三、產品升級為系統層級，成本優勢築高護城河  
公司於法說表示，頻率元件已不再只是「器件」 ，其提供的頻率也
不再只是一項規格，而是整個  AI 生態系的系統級條件，如同系
統的心臟。公司四年前即提出生態系概念，三年前進一步歸納出
「運算、連接、同步」三大主軸，三者都必須仰賴頻率元件。展
望未來三至五年，一個確定的趨勢是：運算越來越分散 ( 散到  
GPU/CPU/DPU/ 光模組等多晶片多節點 )、傳輸越來越收斂，運算
能力越強、延遲要求越低，對同步的要求也越來越嚴苛這正是頻
率元件需求量價都增長的原因，同步發生的結構性來源，且無單
一器件規格可滿足整個生態系。

石英的技術優勢正對應此趨勢。高速傳輸要求極低抖動與極低相

### Page 7

Company Report 
公司研究報告

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

7 
位噪聲 ， 石英天生的高 Q 值恰能提供 ； 在同步層面 ，TCXO、OCXO 
則負責確保各運算節點的時序一致。相較之下，晶技的一條龍自
製製程，在交期、品質、供應能力與客戶關係上均較純設計廠具
優勢，亦為客戶採用時的重要考量。公司為全球營收第一的石英
供應商，主要競爭者為台系、日系與中國同業；就日系而言雙方
各有優缺，關鍵在於如何掌握；在中國市場，晶技本身即為當地
供應鏈一環，並居前兩大。

對於近期市場關注的  MEMS 是否取代石英，公司認為只有些許
部分影響，是競爭者但不是替代者，石英成本優勢太大。其論點
在於： MEMS 屬半導體製程、僅做設計而其餘委外，定位落在  
clock/ 振盪器範疇，並非能取代全部石英的技術；純被動的石英晶
體僅需兩隻接腳 (2-pin) 、單顆約  4~5 美分， MEMS 因結構上必
含 ASIC ，故  MEMS 應對標  TCXO/XO 一類主動產品而非裸晶
體。就成本結構而言， MEMS 屬半導體，一次  12 吋光罩投入動
輒約上百萬美元、失敗難以回收，石英的成本優勢就顯現。公司
並指出， MEMS 業者多年虧損、近年才因量體出現而轉盈；其早
期能切入光模組，主要憑藉美系光環、強勢行銷與龐大通路，惟
一旦進入亞洲  OEM 供應鏈，客戶對單一供應商的疑慮將帶來第
二供應商的替代空間，晶技如何於早期切入客戶反為需補強的課
題。整體而言，公司視  MEMS 為競爭者而非取代者。

供應韌性則是第三道護城河，並將地緣政治風險轉為優勢。公司
於台灣、中國、日本、印尼四地布局，策略上讓各廠皆能生產全
面性產品以分散風險；若任一廠區發生供應鏈問題，另一廠區可
於 3~6 個月內承接。其中印尼泗水廠即為因應「非紅 (NCNT) 」
供應鏈需求而設，自設立起即以符合車用  IATF 16949 為目標，
具備供應  Tier 1 的資格；面對美國對中國潛在管制，晶技可同時
提供中國國產化與非紅兩種選項，彈性因應。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估 
一、預估 2026 年稅後  EPS 為 7.17 元， YoY+36.2% 
以 2Q26 實際為基礎逐季推估，驅動因子包含雙 A 放量、光模組
拉貨 (7 月光模組占比升 )、手機 /消費性結構性收斂，並納入季節性
(4Q 營收低於  3Q) 。預估  3Q26 營收  41.2 億元， QoQ +11.4% 、
YoY +18.0% ；4Q26 營收  39.2 億元， QoQ -4.8% 、YoY +18.0% 。
全年營收  150.8 億元、 YoY +13.0% 。毛利率明顯逐季走升 (1Q 
32.33% →2Q 33.11% →3Q 35.80% →4Q 36.20%) ，全年  34.5%(H1 
32.74% 、H2 顯著回升 )。改善由產品組合驅動， AI 與車用高毛利
占比拉高；公司明確表示關鍵非漲價 (僅反映貴金屬成本 )，健康的

1 群益投顧 
聯亞 (3081  TT) 公布 與美、日 InP 基 板 大 廠 簽 訂 四 年 供 應 長 約 ， 看 好AI 驅 動 高 速 光 通
訊 需 求 ， 並 再 次 投 資 買 入 德 國 大 廠MOCVD 設備，顯示持續擴產 CW Laser 的決心 ；
展望後勢， 近期 InP 基 板 已 重 新 獲 得 供 應 ， 預 期 在 中 國 政 府 放 鬆 管 制 的 狀 況 下 ，InP
基 板 不 再 是 限 制 營 運 擴 張 的 因 素 ， 受 到AI Scale -Out 需 求 帶 動 ，CW Chip 與 CW Epi
營 收 皆 持 續 成 長 ， 維 持 Buy 建議 。

研 究 員 ： 劉 宜 和   
yiho.liu@capital.com.tw

聯亞 公布 InP 基 板 長 約 ， 並 加 碼 投 資 MOCVD 設 備 加 大 擴 產 力 道：  
聯亞 公 布 與 美 、 日 InP 基 板 大 廠 簽 訂 四 年 供 應 長 約 ， 看 好 AI 驅 動 高 速 光 通
訊 需 求，並 再 次 投 資 9.9 億元 買 入 德 國 大 廠 MOCVD 設備 加 大 擴 產 力 道，顯
示 看 好 未 來 AI 基 建 成 長 力 道 ，持 續 擴 產 CW Laser 的 決 心 。  
展望 3Q26 ， AI 基 礎 建 設 持 續 擴 張 ， 推 升 Scale -Out 連 線 需 求 ， 由 於
Sipho -Based 光 收 發 模 組 相 對 EML 光 收 發 模 組 具 有 成 本 優 勢 ， 進 一 步 推 升
CW 雷 射 的 需 求；由 於 新 竹 的 CW 雷 射 後 段 合 作 夥 伴 經 歷 多 次 嘗 試 後 終 於 順
利 擴 產 ， 推 動 CW Chip 出 貨 量 大 幅 增 長 ， 持 續 推 升 營 收 成 長 ， 預 估 3Q26
營收 15.36 億 元，QoQ+25.81% ，稅 後 淨 利 5.55 億 元，QoQ+18.52% ，EPS5.45
元 。  
展望 2026 年， AI 需 求 帶 動 CW Laser 需 求 持 續 增 加 ， 受 惠 於 新 增 北 美 CSP
客戶以及 CW Epi 客 戶 需 求 大 幅 增 加 ， 預 期2026 年 矽 光 相 關 營 收 可 望 為 2025
年的 3 倍 ， 隨 著 公 司 持 續 擴 產 ，2027 年 底 產 能 將 較 2026 年 底 成 長 100% 以
上 ， 且 進 一 步 帶 動 2028 年 產 能 擴 張 ； 藉 著 與 住 友 電 工 簽 訂 5 年 長 期 磷 化 銦
基板合約，將可望降低中國管制稀土帶來的不確定性，預估 2026 年營收
53.19 億 元，YoY+141.48% ，稅 後 淨 利 19.53 億 元，YoY+355.82% ，EPS19.18
元 。  
近期 InP 基 板 已 重 新 獲 得 供 應 ， 預 期 在 中 國 政 府 放 鬆 管 制 的 狀 況 下 ，InP 基
板 不 再 是 限 制 營 運 擴 張 的 因 素 ， 受 到 AI Scale -Out 需 求 帶 動 ，CW Chip 與
CW Epi 營 收 皆 持 續 成 長 ， 維 持 Buy 建議 。

產業速報

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投資評等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較 3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為三個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」 及 「 買 進 」 均 有 upside 目 標 價 。 兩 者 差 別 僅 在 於 ， 首 次 評 等
時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「買進」及「強力買進」之投資評等，就是責任的
開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ，
直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。

本次報告：法說會

安勤 (3479 TT) 
醫療仍為主要成長動能

永豐觀點 
心導管伺服器除既有機種出貨穩健外 ， 下一代新機種有望從單板產品逐步轉向系統
整合，目前已確定取得訂單，未來 CT 與 MRI 潛在市場成長空間大。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議：主係因(1) 醫療產品出貨量符合預期，2026 年營收將成長
逾 20%，未來新機種有望從單板產品逐步轉向系統整合，擴大營收貢獻。此外，
超音波設備客戶出貨；(2) 零售及半導體設備皆有接獲新專案，整體接單狀況佳。
評價方面，以 2027 年稅後 EPS 9.22 元(稀釋後)，目前本益比 14 倍，歷史本益比
區間為 10-16 倍，考量醫療及零售訂單穩健成長外，其餘如半導體設備等訂單將
貢獻營收 ， 故維持買進的投資建議 ， 上修目標價至148 元(16 X 2027 稀釋後 EPS)。
